Group 21
601. [Backend release tests] ✅ Release HTTP routes suite passes via `npm exec --workspace backend-nodejs vitest run test/releaseHttpRoutes.test.js`.
602. [Backend release tests] Applied automatic spy cleanup (`clearMocks`, `restoreMocks`, `mockReset`) in `backend-nodejs/vitest.config.mjs` to prevent cross-test contamination between release route scenarios.
603. [Backend release tests] Validation and Redis failures logged during the suite are intentional assertions against boundary cases; no production outage is implied while running against in-memory fixtures.
604. [Backend release tests] The SQLite `setup_runs` table remains intentionally absent in tests—its missing-table error is mocked for resilience checks and needs no further remediation.
